if "static int sensor_read_with_retry(" in code:

/*
 * Retry transient -EAGAIN without discarding
 * the entire sampling period.
 *
 * Maximum additional wait: 50 ms.
 */
static int sensor_read_with_retry(
    SensorThreadContext *ctx,
    SensorData *sample)
{
    int result = sensor_read_device(sample);

    for (unsigned int i = 0;
         result == -EAGAIN && i < 5U &&
         !atomic_load(ctx->stop_requested);
         ++i) {

        struct timespec pause = {
            .tv_sec = 0,
            .tv_nsec = 10000000L
        };

        ctx->eagain_count++;

        while (nanosleep(&pause, &pause) == -1) {
            if (errno != EINTR)
                return -errno;

            if (atomic_load(ctx->stop_requested))
                return -EINTR;
        }

        if (atomic_load(ctx->stop_requested))
            return -EINTR;

        result = sensor_read_device(sample);
    }

[7m    return result;[27m
[7m}[27m

python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path

path = Path("app/src/sensor_thread.c")
code = path.read_text()

if "static int sensor_read_with_retry(" in code:
    raise SystemExit("Retry function already exists; not patching again.")

old_call = "result = sensor_read_device(&sample);"
marker = "/* Add milliseconds to an absolute deadline */"

if code.count(old_call) != 1:
    raise SystemExit("ERROR: Expected one sensor_read_device call.")

if code.count(marker) != 1:
    raise SystemExit("ERROR: Cannot find insertion location.")

helper = r'''
/*
 * Retry transient -EAGAIN without discarding
 * the entire sampling period.
 *
 * Maximum additional wait: 50 ms.
 */
static int sensor_read_with_retry(
    SensorThreadContext *ctx,
    SensorData *sample)
{
    int result = sensor_read_device(sample);

    for (unsigned int i = 0;
         result == -EAGAIN && i < 5U &&
         !atomic_load(ctx->stop_requested);
         ++i) {

        struct timespec pause = {
            .tv_sec = 0,
            .tv_nsec = 10000000L
        };

        ctx->eagain_count++;

        while (nanosleep(&pause, &pause) == -1) {
            if (errno != EINTR)
                return -errno;

            if (atomic_load(ctx->stop_requested))
                return -EINTR;
        }

        if (atomic_load(ctx->stop_requested))
            return -EINTR;

        result = sensor_read_device(sample);
    }

    return result;
}

'''

code = code.replace(marker, helper + marker, 1)

code = code.replace(
    old_call,
    "result = sensor_read_with_retry(ctx, &sample);",
    1
)

path.write_text(code)

print("PATCH SENSOR RETRY: PASS")
PY

python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path

path = Path("app/src/sensor_thread.c")
code = path.read_text()

if "static int sensor_read_with_retry(" in code:
    raise SystemExit("Retry function already exists; not patching again.")

old_call = "result = sensor_read_device(&sample);"
marker = "/* Add milliseconds to an absolute deadline */"

if code.count(old_call) != 1:
    raise SystemExit("ERROR: Expected one sensor_read_device call.")

if code.count(marker) != 1:
    raise SystemExit("ERROR: Cannot find insertion location.")

helper = r'''
/*
 * Retry transient -EAGAIN without discarding
 * the entire sampling period.
 *
 * Maximum additional wait: 50 ms.
 */
static int sensor_read_with_retry(
    SensorThreadContext *ctx,
    SensorData *sample)
{
    int result = sensor_read_device(sample);

    for (unsigned int i = 0;
         result == -EAGAIN && i < 5U &&
         !atomic_load(ctx->stop_requested);
         ++i) {

        struct timespec pause = {
            .tv_sec = 0,
            .tv_nsec = 10000000L
        };

        ctx->eagain_count++;

        while (nanosleep(&pause, &pause) == -1) {
            if (errno != EINTR)
                return -errno;

            if (atomic_load(ctx->stop_requested))
                return -EINTR;
        }

        if (atomic_load(ctx->stop_requested))
            return -EINTR;

        result = sensor_read_device(sample);
    }

    return result;
}

'''

code = code.replace(marker, helper + marker, 1)

code = code.replace(
    old_call,
    "result = sensor_read_with_retry(ctx, &sample);",
    1
)

path.write_text(code)

print("PATCH SENSOR RETRY: PASS")
PY

]133;C\]666;vte.shell.preexec!\===== E.6.2 SENSOR THREAD SOURCE =====
]133;C\]666;vte.shell.preexec!\   150	
   151	    for (unsigned int i = 0;
   152	         result == -EAGAIN && i < 20U &&
   153	         !atomic_load(ctx->stop_requested);
   154	         ++i) {
   155	
   156	        struct timespec pause = {
   157	            .tv_sec = 0,
   158	            .tv_nsec = 10000000L
   159	        };
   160	
   161	        ctx->eagain_count++;
   162	
   163	        while (nanosleep(&pause, &pause) == -1) {
   164	            if (errno != EINTR)
   165	                return -errno;
   166	
   167	            if (atomic_load(ctx->stop_requested))
   168	                return -EINTR;
   169	        }
   170	
   171	        if (atomic_load(ctx->stop_requested))
   172	            return -EINTR;
   173	
   174	        result = sensor_read_device(sample);
   175	    }
   176	
   177	    return result;
   178	}
   179	
   180	/* Add milliseconds to an absolute deadline */
   181	static void sensor_add_ms(
   182	    struct timespec *timepoint,
   183	    unsigned int milliseconds)
   184	{
   185	    timepoint->tv_sec += milliseconds / 1000U;
   186	
   187	    timepoint->tv_nsec +=
   188	        (long)(milliseconds % 1000U) * 1000000L;
   189	
   190	    if (timepoint->tv_nsec >= 1000000000L) {
   191	        timepoint->tv_sec++;
   192	        timepoint->tv_nsec -= 1000000000L;
   193	    }
   194	}
   195	
   196	/* Main Sensor Thread */
   197	void *sms_sensor_thread(void *arg)
   198	{
   199	    SensorThreadContext *ctx = arg;
   200	    struct timespec next_wakeup;
   201	    SensorData sample;
   202	
   203	    unsigned int seed;
   204	    int result;
   205	
   206	    if (ctx == NULL || ctx->buffer == NULL ||
   207	        ctx->config == NULL ||
   208	        ctx->stop_requested == NULL)
   209	        return NULL;
   210	
   211	    if (ctx->config->interval_ms == 0) {
   212	        ctx->read_errors++;
   213	        return NULL;
   214	    }
   215	
   216	    if (!ctx->use_mock) {
   217	        result = sensor_configure_driver(
   218	            ctx->config->interval_ms
   219	        );
   220	
   221	        if (result != 0) {
   222	            fprintf(stderr,
   223	                    "[SENSOR] ioctl failed: %d\n",
   224	                    result);
   225	            ctx->read_errors++;
   226	            atomic_store(ctx->stop_requested, true);
   227	            return NULL;
   228	        }
   229	    }
   230	
   231	    seed = (unsigned int)time(NULL);
   232	
   233	    if (clock_gettime(
   234	            CLOCK_MONOTONIC,
   235	            &next_wakeup) != 0) {
   236	        ctx->read_errors++;
   237	        atomic_store(ctx->stop_requested, true);
   238	        return NULL;
   239	    }
   240	
   241	    while (!atomic_load(ctx->stop_requested)) {
   242	
   243	        if (ctx->use_mock) {
   244	            result = sensor_mock_sample(&sample, &seed);
   245	        } else {
   246	            result = sensor_read_with_retry(ctx, &sample);
   247	        }
   248	
   249	        if (result == 0) {
   250	
   251	            int push_result =
   252	                sms_buffer_push(ctx->buffer, &sample);
   253	
   254	            if (push_result == 0) {
   255	                ctx->samples_produced++;
   256	
   257	                if (ctx->config->verbose) {
   258	                    printf(
   259	                        "[SENSOR] %llu,%.2f,%.2f\n",
   260	                        (unsigned long long)
   261	                            sample.timestamp_ms,
   262	                        sample.temperature,
   263	                        sample.humidity
   264	                    );
   265	                    fflush(stdout);
   266	                }
   267	
   268	            } else if (push_result == -ENOSPC) {
   269	                ctx->overflow_count++;
   270	
   271	                fprintf(stderr,
   272	                        "[SENSOR] buffer full\n");
   273	
   274	            } else if (push_result == -ECANCELED) {
   275	                break;
   276	
   277	            } else {
   278	                ctx->read_errors++;
   279	            }
   280	
   281	        } else if (result == -EAGAIN) {
   282	            ctx->eagain_count++;
   283	
   284	        } else if (result == -EINTR) {
   285	            /* Retry on the next sampling cycle */
   286	
   287	        } else {
   288	            ctx->read_errors++;
   289	
   290	            fprintf(stderr,
   291	                    "[SENSOR] read error: %d\n",
   292	                    result);
   293	        }
   294	
   295	        sensor_add_ms(
   296	            &next_wakeup,
   297	            ctx->config->interval_ms
   298	        );
   299	
   300	        while (!atomic_load(ctx->stop_requested)) {
   301	
   302	            result = clock_nanosleep(
   303	                CLOCK_MONOTONIC,
   304	                TIMER_ABSTIME,
   305	                &next_wakeup,
   306	                NULL
   307	            );
   308	
   309	            if (result == 0) {
   310	                struct timespec actual;
   311	                if (ctx->jitter_us != NULL &&
   312	                    ctx->jitter_count < ctx->jitter_capacity &&
   313	                    clock_gettime(CLOCK_MONOTONIC, &actual) == 0) {
   314	                    int64_t delta_ns =
   315	                        (int64_t)(actual.tv_sec - next_wakeup.tv_sec) *
   316	                            1000000000LL +
   317	                        (int64_t)(actual.tv_nsec - next_wakeup.tv_nsec);
   318	                    ctx->jitter_us[ctx->jitter_count++] =
   319	                        delta_ns / 1000LL;
   320	                }
   321	                break;
   322	            }
   323	
   324	            if (result != EINTR) {
   325	                ctx->read_errors++;
   326	                break;
   327	            }
   328	        }
   329	    }
   330	
   331	    return NULL;
   332	}

   232

   286

   292	                    result);

   326	                break;

python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path

p = Path("app/src/sensor_thread.c")
s = p.read_text()

if "SCHED_DIAG missed_deadlines=" in s:
    raise SystemExit("Deadline diagnostic already installed.")

loop_anchor = """    while (!atomic_load(ctx->stop_requested)) {

        if (ctx->use_mock) {"""

old_deadline = """        sensor_add_ms(
            &next_wakeup,
            ctx->config->interval_ms
        );

        while (!atomic_load(ctx->stop_requested)) {"""

new_deadline = """        sensor_add_ms(
            &next_wakeup,
            ctx->config->interval_ms
        );

        /*
         * Skip expired absolute deadlines.
         * Keep missed deadlines separate from wake-up jitter.
         */
        {
            struct timespec before_sleep;

            if (clock_gettime(CLOCK_MONOTONIC,
                              &before_sleep) != 0) {
                ctx->read_errors++;
                atomic_store(ctx->stop_requested, true);
                break;
            }

            if (ctx->config->interval_ms == 0) {
                ctx->read_errors++;
                atomic_store(ctx->stop_requested, true);
                break;
            }

            while (
                next_wakeup.tv_sec < before_sleep.tv_sec ||
                (next_wakeup.tv_sec == before_sleep.tv_sec &&
                 next_wakeup.tv_nsec <= before_sleep.tv_nsec)
            ) {
                sensor_add_ms(
                    &next_wakeup,
                    ctx->config->interval_ms
                );

                missed_deadlines++;
            }
        }

        while (!atomic_load(ctx->stop_requested)) {"""

tail_anchor = "    return NULL;\n}"

if s.count(loop_anchor) != 1:
    raise SystemExit("STOP: Cannot locate sensor main loop.")

if s.count(old_deadline) != 1:
    raise SystemExit("STOP: Cannot locate deadline update.")

if not s.rstrip().endswith(tail_anchor):
    raise SystemExit("STOP: Unexpected end of sensor thread.")

s = s.replace(
    loop_anchor,
    """    unsigned long long missed_deadlines = 0;

""" + loop_anchor,
    1
)

s = s.replace(old_deadline, new_deadline, 1)

head, tail = s.rsplit(tail_anchor, 1)

s = (
    head
    + '    printf("SCHED_DIAG missed_deadlines=%llu\\n",\n'
      '           missed_deadlines);\n\n'
    + tail_anchor
    + tail
)

p.write_text(s)

print("DEADLINE FIX: INSTALLED")
print("MISSED DEADLINE COUNTER: INSTALLED")
PY
